# Commands · R · part 8

1,435 entries, research-analyze to resume. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **research-analyze**: Enter the `analyze` stage (Phase A → B → C). Phase A dispatches results-analyst in plan-only mode; Phase C chains F-1 results-analyst → F-2 codex-reviewer then emits a Report pair. Trigger phrase required to advance from B to C. Final stage gate is codex-reviewer (F-2). ([youai058/research_hub](https://github.com/youai058/research_hub/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/research-analyze.md))
- **research-and-self-factcheck**: Research a topic, then adversarially fact-check your own claims ([dmavridi6478/business](https://github.com/dmavridi6478/business/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/research-and-self-factcheck.md))
- **research-and-store**: Research a topic using Perplexity and store findings in the vault. ([motorthings/clode](https://github.com/motorthings/clode/blob/HEAD/project-templates/obsidian-vault/commands/research-and-store.md) · ★1)

- **research-brief**: Thin preset over the Claudify Analyst engine: the fast lane for one question. `/research-brief "&lt;question&gt;"` = `/research` STAGE 1 ONLY, in EVIDENCE-MAP mode by default (no framing card unless offered), stage-1 budget (well under one dollar): the decision frame, the MECE tree with cruxes tagged, the hypotheses, and the ranked frontier, with NO evidence collection. The output tells you what the que ([gannino/your-assistant](https://github.com/gannino/your-assistant/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/research-brief.md))
